- Owner: DC600 vendor liaison / integration lead
- Decision/source: OD-004; SRC-040
- Reason: SIM builds to Appendix A (INT32 big-endian as stated). LIVE still needs Siemens/EVCS confirm of exact addresses and INT32 byte/word order.
- Missing: Siemens EVCS holdings document; INT32 word-order confirm on the as-built charger; Master/slave as-built confirm; PMS update rate (60 Hz vs 0.5-5)

### Blocker: en-ems-v118-export-not-field-ready

- Owner: integration lead
- Decision/source: OD-002; SRC-038
- Reason: This export is a register dictionary. The Comm sheet makes the EMS the Modbus slave. It is not an approved poll of the installed battery.
- Missing: Original workbook hash match against SRC-014; Installed host, port, and device address; int64 rows, which this schema cannot carry; row 76 Total Charge Amount at 0x110B~0x110E type int64; row 77 Total Discharge Amount at 0x110F~0x1112 type int64
